# PostgreSQL database contract: Rust reference for Go apiserver

Phase 1 P1-05 evidence. Source of truth for schema and repository migration. Evidence generated from forward migrations and `apiserver/src/schema.rs`; `down.sql` is rollback-only and not used as final shape.

## Counts and scope

| Item | Count | Evidence |
|---|---:|---|
| Forward migration files | 44 | `apiserver/migrations/**/up.sql`; system and tenant sets embedded by `apiserver/src/diesel_pool/migrations.rs:8-20,26-57` |
| Final Diesel tables | 137 | `apiserver/src/schema.rs` table macros; includes 111 logical/base tables plus 26 partition children/default-month partitions |
| Migration-only tables absent from Diesel schema | 4 | `employee_department_history` and 3 econtract tables; migration origins `tenant/20260609000001_init_tenant/up.sql:894-901`, `20260624000000_create_econtract_tables/up.sql:2-51` |
| Repository query families | 80 implementation modules | `apiserver/src/repositories/diesel_*.rs`, nested repository modules, and `workspace_stats_repository.rs` |
| Raw SQL files | 30 | `apiserver/src/repositories/sql/` |

Counts exclude three root placeholder `up.sql` files that contain comments only as operations, but count them as migration files. `employee_department_history` and three econtract tables exist in migrations but are absent from generated Diesel schema; they are listed under unknown/dead coverage below.

## Database split and migration execution

- System migrations run against primary/system database through `SYSTEM_MIGRATIONS` at `apiserver/src/diesel_pool/migrations.rs:2-23`. Tenant migrations run per workspace database through `TENANT_MIGRATIONS` at `apiserver/src/diesel_pool/migrations.rs:26-57`; test path runs system then tenant at `:60-89`.
- Repository tenant connection obtains workspace-specific pool with `tenant_transaction_conn` at `apiserver/src/diesel_pool/core.rs:96-107`; session connection uses `tenant_session_conn` at `:109-118`.
- `with_tenant_tx` acquires tenant connection then wraps callback in Diesel transaction at `apiserver/src/diesel_pool/core.rs:70-92`: callback success commits; callback error rolls back. No repository calls `commit()`/`rollback()` directly (search evidence `apiserver/src/repositories`); Go must preserve this boundary.

Operation notation: line references are exact `up.sql` lines. `CREATE`/`ALTER`/constraint/index/function/trigger entries are grouped only when one migration contains many repetitive statements; order remains source order. Empty root migrations are retained explicitly.

### `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260614000002_payroll_scope_to_employment/up.sql`
- `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260614000002_payroll_scope_to_employment/up.sql:6-16` UPDATE workspace_config_entries SET scope_type = 'employment', scope_id = emp.id FROM employments emp WHERE wce.scope_type = 'employee' AND wce.scope_id = emp.employee_id AND wce.config_key LIKE 'payroll.%' AND emp.status = 'active' AND emp.deleted_at IS NULL (data migration: employee scope → employment scope).
- `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260614000002_payroll_scope_to_employment/up.sql:20-33` UPDATE workspace_config_entries SET scope_type = 'employment', scope_id = sub.employment_id FROM (SELECT DISTINCT ON (e.employee_id) e.id AS employment_id, e.employee_id FROM employments e WHERE e.deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY e.employee_id, e.start_date DESC) sub WHERE wce.scope_type = 'employee' AND wce.scope_id = sub.employee_id AND wce.config_key LIKE 'payroll.%' (orphan fallback to most recent employment).

## Final table shape

`schema.rs` types are Diesel/PostgreSQL mappings: `Uuid`, `Varchar`, `Text`, `Int4`, `Int8`, `Int2`, `Float8`, `Numeric`, `Bool`, `Date`, `Time`, `Timestamptz`, `Jsonb`, `Inet`, and `Array<...>`. `workspace_id` means tenant row key. A tenant database is already physically isolated; rows without `workspace_id` have `physical tenant DB; no row tenant column`. Soft-delete is only `deleted_at`; `is_active`/status are lifecycle flags, not implicit soft-delete.

### Schema/migration discrepancies

- `employee_department_history` is created at `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260609000001_init_tenant/up.sql:894-901`, but has no `schema.rs` table macro. Repository `get_employee_department_history` and batch form query `employments` joined to `employees`, not this table (`apiserver/src/repositories/diesel_department_repository.rs:610-642,645-686`). Treat migration table as legacy/dead unless Go decision says preserve it.
- `employer_econtract_configs`, `employer_econtract_routing_rules`, and `econtract_envelopes` are created at `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260624000000_create_econtract_tables/up.sql:2-51`, but are absent from `schema.rs` and have no Rust repository references found. Mark `UNKNOWN — needs decision` before Go migration; do not silently drop schema.

## Repository query families

One row equals one implementation module/family; methods list exact Rust symbols and definition lines. Strategy is REQUIRED for every row. `sqlc query` covers stable Diesel CRUD/list/get DSL; `pgx manual` covers raw SQL, lock/transaction-heavy workflows, dynamic aggregates, or missing generated schema; `batch/prefetch` covers explicit batch APIs and avoids per-row query loops.

### Query family categories and required behavior

- **List/get:** Diesel `.filter`, joins, `.first`/`.load`, stable `.order`; preserve workspace predicates and `deleted_at.is_null` where shown in family evidence. Typical strategy `sqlc query` unless raw SQL/lock row.
- **Create/update:** Diesel `insert_into`, `update`, `on_conflict`, `.returning`; preserve `with_tenant_tx` or explicit transaction callback. Strategy `sqlc query`; multi-step create/update uses `pgx manual` when row lock/transaction choreography exists.
- **Soft-delete/delete:** hard `delete` is used by several repositories; only `deleted_at` filters qualify as soft-delete. Do not translate `is_active` to deletion. Strategy `sqlc query` for one statement; `pgx manual` for cascade/transaction operations.
- **Bulk/batch:** `eq_any`, batch insert/upsert, and methods with `_batch`/`bulk_`; strategy `batch/prefetch`, not a loop of single-row queries.
- **Report/aggregate:** `workspace_stats_repository.rs` and recruitment SQL use `COUNT`, `GROUP BY`, date ranges, joins, and SQL files; strategy `pgx manual` (or sqlc only after exact SQL is frozen).

## Filters, ordering, pagination, and soft-delete contract

- Workspace filter syntax appears as Diesel `.filter(<table>::workspace_id.eq(workspace_id))` / `.eq_any(...)`; representative exact evidence: `apiserver/src/repositories/diesel_department_repository.rs:135,172,213,259`, `diesel_employee_repository/search.rs:158,199,227`, `diesel_leave_request_repository/queries.rs:26,51,75,109`, `diesel_time_entry_repository.rs:94,117,151,184,207`. Per-table references in final-shape table cite repository lines containing table/filter usage; physical tenant isolation still applies to every tenant repository call.
- Soft-delete predicate is `.filter(<table>::deleted_at.is_null())`; examples `apiserver/src/repositories/diesel_department_repository.rs:136,173,214,260`, `diesel_custom_field_repository.rs:86,108,142,211`, `diesel_employment_repository.rs:85,111,157,207,306`, and `diesel_leave_policy_repository.rs:60,311,376,417`. Mutations may set `deleted_at` or execute hard delete; inspect exact method in family table.
- Pagination uses `.limit(...)` plus `.offset(...)` in repository families; ordering uses `.order(...)`. Representative exact lines: `apiserver/src/repositories/diesel_attendance_adjustment_repository.rs:222-224`, `diesel_leave_request_repository/queries.rs:52,83,156`, `diesel_time_entry_repository.rs:120,153,261`, `diesel_employee_repository/segments.rs:172,178,389-403`, `diesel_workspace_repository/workspace_members.rs:153-154` and `workspace_roles.rs:293-294`. Preserve ordering before limit/offset and count-query pairing.
- Locking: `FOR UPDATE`/`.for_update()` occurs in approval action, infra jobs, outbox jobs, monitor alerts, and staging logs (`apiserver/src/repositories/diesel_approval_workflow_repository.rs:793-799`, `diesel_infra_job_repository.rs:125-130`, `diesel_outbox_job_repository.rs:220,303`, `diesel_staging_log_repository.rs:347-359`). Go must use `pgx manual` or sqlc transaction + `FOR UPDATE`; never split lock and mutation across transactions.

## Raw SQL in `repositories/sql/`

Raw SQL file count: **30**. Files are compiled into Rust with `include_str!`; recruitment loader evidence `apiserver/src/repositories/diesel_employee_repository/recruitment.rs:211,245,256,266,276,287,318,328,338,348,357,407,435,463,491,519,547`; workspace stats loader evidence `apiserver/src/repositories/workspace_stats_repository.rs:247,284,369,561,624,650,686,722,758,868,930,992,1054,1105`. Keep SQL text as Go `sqlc` queries only after result structs/parameters match; otherwise `pgx manual`.

## Transaction boundary by query family

- Default tenant query boundary: repository method calls `with_tenant_tx` (family table records source lines), which obtains workspace pool and wraps callback in one transaction; commit on `Ok`, rollback on `Err` (`apiserver/src/diesel_pool/core.rs:85-92`).
- Explicit Diesel transaction boundary: methods with `.transaction(...)` listed in family table; examples `apply_approval_action_transactional` (`apiserver/src/repositories/diesel_approval_workflow_repository.rs:777-803`), employee CRUD (`diesel_employee_repository/crud.rs:35,153,246,344`), lifecycle template/checklist (`diesel_lifecycle_checklist_repository.rs:52,161,522`), shift version/archive (`diesel_shift_repository.rs:64,334,503`), and workspace create/clear (`diesel_workspace_repository/workspace_crud.rs:52,152`).
- Session-only exception: signature repository obtains `tenant_session_conn` (`apiserver/src/repositories/diesel_signature_repository.rs:31,47,63,80,99`) and performs statement-level work without repository-local transaction wrapper. Go strategy must use session pool semantics where required.
- Call-site transactions outside repository: leave approval/submission (`apiserver/src/services/leave_service/approval.rs:58`, `submission.rs:163`) and pairing recalculation/range/single paths (`apiserver/src/services/pairing/service_helpers.rs:191,274`, `service_range.rs:63,233`, `service_single.rs:185,282`). Go service layer must not add nested transactions without checking Diesel behavior.

## N+1 risks found

Static scan found repository calls inside loops. These are concrete risk points, not proof every request executes N+1; prefer existing batch APIs and verify query counts:
- `apiserver/src/handlers/employee_assignments.rs:94,229,283` — loops employment rows and calls get/update/delete repository methods.
- `apiserver/src/handlers/employees.rs:657` — loops employments and calls create repository method.
- `apiserver/src/handlers/overtime_request.rs:124` — loops employee IDs and calls create method.
- `apiserver/src/handlers/payroll/payslips.rs:225` — loops payslips and calls get method.
- `apiserver/src/services/approval_workflow/engine.rs:110,153,175` — loops instances/requests and calls get methods.
- `apiserver/src/services/employee_service/employment.rs:41,89` — loops employments and calls create/update methods.
- `apiserver/src/services/holiday_leave_service.rs:132` — loops active employments and calls get method.
- `apiserver/src/services/leave_accrual_service/qualifying_days.rs:117` — loops employees and calls get method.
- `apiserver/src/services/leave_import_service.rs:174,178` — loops employments/employees and calls get methods.
- `apiserver/src/services/leave_service/conflict.rs:92,216` — loops requests and calls get methods.
- `apiserver/src/services/overtime_request_service.rs:27` — loops employees and calls get/create methods.
- `apiserver/src/services/payroll_service/batch_chunk.rs:40,236` — loops employment histories and calls get methods.
- `apiserver/src/services/payroll_service/batch_helpers.rs:62` — loops policies and calls get method.
- `apiserver/src/services/payroll_service/batch_orchestrator.rs:36` — loops employment IDs and calls get method.
- `apiserver/src/services/payroll_service/fetch.rs:89,186` — loops time entries/members and calls get methods.
- `apiserver/src/services/policy_resolver_service.rs:235` — loops employees and calls get method.
- `apiserver/src/services/report_service/absences.rs:55` — loops employments and calls get method.
- `apiserver/src/services/termination_service.rs:102` — loops employments and calls get method.

- `apiserver/src/services/custom_field_service.rs:307` — loops values and calls get method.
Existing batch/prefetch evidence includes `get_employee_department_history_batch` (`apiserver/src/repositories/diesel_department_repository.rs:645-673`), employee/timezone/policy/payroll batch methods, `eq_any` queries, and batch upserts. Go implementation MUST choose `batch/prefetch` for these paths.

## Tables without repository query coverage

Direct non-test repository reference scan identified 32 tables in `schema.rs` with no direct repository queries in `apiserver/src/repositories/`. Categorized by reason:

### 1. PostgreSQL partition child tables (26 tables)
PostgreSQL range-partition child tables are routed transparently via the parent tables (`attendance_staging_logs` and `audit_logs`). Repositories intentionally query only the parent table name, never partition children:
- `attendance_staging_logs_default` (origin `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260609000001_init_tenant/up.sql:430`)
- `attendance_staging_logs_y2026m01` through `y2026m12` (12 monthly tables; origins `:442,454,466,478,490,502,514,526,538,550,562,574`)
- `audit_logs_default` (origin `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260609000001_init_tenant/up.sql:603`)
- `audit_logs_y2026m01` through `y2026m12` (12 monthly tables; origins `:619,635,651,667,683,699,715,731,747,763,779,795`)

### 2. Feature models without dedicated repositories (4 tables)
These tables have Diesel models in `apiserver/src/models/` but no corresponding repository module; operations are handled inline or feature is in progress:
- `device_enrollments` — model in `apiserver/src/models/diesel/device_enrollments.rs`; schema origin `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260719000000_biometric_enrollment/up.sql:52`.
- `employee_biometric_templates` — model in `apiserver/src/models/diesel/employee_biometric_templates.rs`; schema origin `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260719000000_biometric_enrollment/up.sql:17`.
- `document_folders` — model in `apiserver/src/models/diesel/document_folders.rs`; handler stub in `apiserver/src/handlers/document_folders.rs`; schema origin `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260620000001_document_management/up.sql:2`.
- `file_acknowledgements` — model in `apiserver/src/models/diesel/file_acknowledgements.rs`; schema origin `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260620000001_document_management/up.sql:35`.

### 3. Obsolete / unreferenced tables (2 tables)
Tables present in schema and initial migration but never queried or populated:
- `payroll_incentive_assignments` — schema origin `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260609000001_init_tenant/up.sql:1239`. Replaced by `payroll_incentive_policies` and `payslip_incentives`.
- `policy_templates` — schema origin `apiserver/migrations/tenant/20260609000001_init_tenant/up.sql:1366`. Replaced by `attendance_policies` and `attendance_policy_definitions`.

### 4. Migration-only tables absent from Diesel schema (4 tables)
- `employee_department_history` — created in `tenant/20260609000001_init_tenant/up.sql:894-901`. Repository queries `employments` joined to `employees` instead (`diesel_department_repository.rs:610-686`).
- `employer_econtract_configs`, `employer_econtract_routing_rules`, `econtract_envelopes` — created in `tenant/20260624000000_create_econtract_tables/up.sql:2-51`. No Rust repository or model exists.

## Go implementation strategy

Strategy assignment complete for all **80** repository implementation families. See query-family table for per-family assignment. Summary:
- `sqlc query`: 30 families.
- `pgx manual`: 20 families.
- `batch/prefetch`: 30 families.
- `sqlc query`: simple CRUD/list/get and stable joins; include explicit workspace and soft-delete predicates in each query.
- `pgx manual`: raw SQL files, dynamic report aggregates, row locks, multi-statement workflows, missing-schema tables, and transaction choreography.
- `batch/prefetch`: methods already accepting ID slices/batch payloads and caller loops flagged above. Use one `ANY($1)`/batch statement or prefetch maps, not one query per row.

- `UNKNOWN — needs decision`: exact PostgreSQL enum type handling in Go. Enum names and values are in `tenant/20260609000001_init_tenant/up.sql` and segment enums in `tenant/20260624000001_work_segments/up.sql`; schema mappings at `apiserver/src/schema.rs:3-59` expose Rust names but not every SQL value.

ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`. Decide whether initial role permissions in Go are provisioned via migrations or an explicit Go bootstrap/seed routine.
